- **mp-code-review**: Review the changes since a fixed point (commit, branch, tag, or merge-base) along two axes — Standards (does the code follow this repo's documented coding standards?) and Spec (does the code match what the originating issue/PRD asked for?). Runs both reviews in parallel sub-agents and reports them side by side. Use when the user wants to review a branch, a PR, work-in-progress changes, or asks to "review since X". Triggers: /lril:mp-code-review, /mp-code-review, /lril-mp-code-review. ([seanrobertwright/claude-ship](https://github.com/seanrobertwright/claude-ship/blob/HEAD/plugins/lril/commands/mp-code-review.md) · 2 copies)

- **mr-review-note**: 출력을 작성하기 전에 공통 지침 `core/rules/review-method-comparison.md`를 읽고 적용한다. 설치 환경에서는 `~/.agent-crew/rules/review-method-comparison.md`를 읽는다. 실제 방법명, 채택/권장/판단 보류 구분, 선택 이유, 얻는 점·감수할 점, 빈 줄과 `방법별 비교` 소제목, 후보별 설명·장단점, 확인 근거와 한계를 포함한다. 방법명이나 후보 개수를 고정하지 않는다. 방법 선택이 없는 항목에는 비교를 만들지 않는다. 이 형식은 보고서·분석·계획·인계·반영 노트의 서술에 적용하며, 원문 인용과 원시 증거, 프로토콜 필드, 상태·반영률·승인·실행 경계는 바꾸지 않는다. - **mv**: Renames or moves tracked paths in the working tree and index, preserving object ids and cached stat data. Pathnames in human and `--markdown` output use the same quoting rules as [`grit status`](../status/) (see **Description** there). The last argument is the destination; earlier arguments are sources. ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/mv.md) · ★598)

- **mv**: Rename or move tracked paths, updating **both** the index and the working tree — faithful to `git mv`. The working file is renamed as-is and the source's index entry (blob id + mode) is copied to the destination; no blob is re-hashed, so an unstaged edit travels with the file while the staged content is preserved. ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/mv.md) · ★4)
